4a1(dkon brtsegs): @#//kyis bla mar byas//bkur stir byas/ri mor byas/mchod par byas so/bcom

4b3(dkon brtsegs): dang bram zer bcas pa'i skye dgu'i 'jig rten 'di dang /lha dang /mi dang lha ma yin du bcas pa'i 'jig rten rnams

5a4(dkon brtsegs): rgyal po'i (ling)kyi grong khyer chen po'i/bya rgod phung po'i ri la bzhugs so//de'i tshe rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyerachen po na/
5a5(dkon brtsegs): khyim bdag tshod dpon bzang skyong zhes bya ba sngon dge ba'i rtsa ba bskyed pa/sngon gyis sangs rgyas la bya ba byas pa/phyugs 
5b1(dkon brtsegs): cing nor mang ba/longs spyod che ba/bdag dbang ba mang ba/nor dang yo byad mang ba/gser dngul mang ba/nor dang 
5b2(dkon brtsegs): 'brus yongs su gang ba'i mdzod dang /bang ba mang ba/nor bu dang /mu tig dang /baidur+ya dang /dung dang /man shel dang /

5b4(dkon brtsegs): ba mang ba zhig gnas te/dge sbyong go'u ta ma shAkya'i rigs las mngon par byung nas/dge slong nyis brgya lnga bcu'i dge slong gi 
5b5(dkon brtsegs): dge 'dun chen po dang /thabs gcig tu yul ma ga ta'i ljongs su rgyu zhing /gshegs te/rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen 
5b6(dkon brtsegs): por byon nas/de nas rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyerachen po nas bya rgod phung po'i ri la bzhugs te/'di ltar bcom ldan 'das 
6a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /de ni de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas rig+sa 
6a2(dkon brtsegs): pa dang zhabs su ldan pa//bde bar gshegs pa/'jigs rten mkhyen pa/skyes bu 'dul ba'i kha lo bsgyur ba/bla na 
6a3(dkon brtsegs): med pa lha dang mi rnams kyi ston pa sangs rgyas bcom ldan 'das yin te//chos ston pa tshangs par spyod pa/thor mar dge ba

6a6(dkon brtsegs): pa'i sgra chigs su bcad pa/rgyal po de lta bu yang mngon par byung ba khyim bdag bzang skyong gis thos nas/de 'di snyam du sem+sa te 
6b1(dkon brtsegs): de bzhin gshegs pa dgra bcom pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i sangs rgyas de lta bu dag mthong na/legs par 'gyur gyis/bdag dge 
6b2(dkon brtsegs): sbyong go'u ta ma de lta bu'i phyir 'gro 'o snyam mo//de nas khyim bdag tshong dpon bzang skyong khyim bdag lnga brgya tsam dang lhan cig tu bcom ldan 
6b3(dkon brtsegs): 'das la lta ba'i phyir/rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen po nas byung ngo//bcom ldan 'das kyang snga dro'i dus kyi tshe/sham thabs

7a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /phyir rgyal po'i khab kyi grong khyer chen por gshegs so//khyim bdag tshong dpon bzang skyong dang /khyim bdag 
7a2(dkon brtsegs): lnga brgya tsam po de dag gis bcom ldan 'das mdzes shing dga' bar gyur pa /dbang po zhi ba dul ba dang /zhi gnas dam pa bsnyes pa dang /glang 
7a3(dkon brtsegs): po dbang po dul ba/mtsho ltar dang zhing gsal ba rnyog pa med pa/lha du mas mchod pa/gnod sbyin du mas mchod pa/mi du mas mchod pa/
7a4(dkon brtsegs): gser gyi kha dog gi skyes bu chen po'i mtshan sum bcu rtsa gnyis dang ldan pa dpe byed bzang po brgyad bcus brgyan pa/me tog gi char chen po rab tu 
7a5(dkon brtsegs): 'babs shing /me tog gi chu bo chen po rab tu 'bebs bzhin du/rin po che sna bdun gyi pad+ma 'dab ma bye ba brgya phrag stongs yod pa la zhabs
7a6(dkon brtsegs): 'dor zhing gshegs pa brgyang ring po nas mthong ste/spyod lam brgya stong pos gshegs pa brgyang ring po nas mthong nas bcom ldan 'das la sems

7b1(dkon brtsegs): phyag 'tshal te/bcom ldan 'das kyi spyan sngar phyogs gcig tu 'khod do//phyogs gcig tu 'khod nas/bcom ldan 'das la khyim bdag bzang skyong 
7b2(dkon brtsegs): dang //khyim bdag lnga brgya tsam po de dag gis 'di skad ces gsol to//bcom ldan 'das go'u ta ma dpal gyis 'khor 'di zil gyis non par mdzad do//
7b3(dkon brtsegs): bcom ldan 'das go'u ta mi'i gzi brjid kyis 'khor 'di zil gyis nonapar mdzad do//bcom ldan 'das go'uta ma 'od gyis 'khor 'di dag zil gyis 
7b4(dkon brtsegs): non par mdzad do//bcom ldana'adas go'uta ma gzugs kyis 'khor 'di zil gyis ni non par mdzad do//bcom

7b7(dkon brtsegs): du sems te//bcom ldan 'das go'utu ta mas ci zhig gzigs na/khab nas khab ma mchis par rab tu byung snyams so//de nas bcom ldan 'das la 
7b8(dkon brtsegs): khyim bdag bzang skyong gis tshigs su bcad pa dag gis mngon par bstod do//rgyal ba'i dpal dang grags dang gzugs dag ni//sngan chad bdag gis 
8a1(dkon brtsegs): ci skad thos pa bas//de las lhag par gser gyi gzugs bzhin du//mdzes dang ldan par lham mer rab tu gda'//
8a2(dkon brtsegs): gzugs ni shin tu mdzes shing ltan sdug/sku'i kha dog gser gyis mdog 'dra bas//skar ma dag gis dbus na zla ba ltar//dge slong mang po'i dbus na 
8a3(dkon brtsegs): mdzes par mdzad//mi mchog gis ni spyi bo ltar mi 'da'//ri rab bzhin du mngon par 'phags par dga'//gtsug gi spyi bo lha'i gdugs bzhin du//
8a4(dkon brtsegs): kun nas byin gyis bzhol bar gyur pa lags//dbu'i skya ni 'jam zhing rab mnyen la//a da rnyil dang stags zil kha dog 'dra//g.yas su 'khyil la 
8a5(dkon brtsegs): shin tu 'jam pa ste//rma bya'i mgrin pa ci bzhin nyid du gda'//smra ba mchog gi phral ba bzhin mnyam pa//khyod kyi sman tshun 'je' khru lta bu ste//'od 
8a6(dkon brtsegs): gsal ba ni skar ma'i rgyal po ltar//rdul bral smin 'tsham spu ni shin tu mdzes//spyan bzangs khyod kyis spyan ni dga' 'gyur ba//'gro ba rnam la mdzes 
8a7(dkon brtsegs): shing mthong na dga' bdag ni lta bas ngoms par mi 'gyur te//mgon po rnam dag spyan la bdag phyag 'tshal//skyes bu dam pa'i gang ni byin gyis mnyam//ji ltar 
8a8(dkon brtsegs): gser gyi ri ltar rab tu gda'//khyed kyis sgros ni bin pa bye ru bzhin//dag cing rnam dag nor bu rin chen 'dra//dul la bgyis ba'i tshems ni mdzes pa ste//
8b1(dkon brtsegs): snang bar byed pa rab tu dkar ba'i mtshems//pad ma'i rtsa ba bzho chu skur 'dra//sra zhing dam la thags bzang shin du dag//tshems dang tshems kyi bar ni shin tu 'byor//
8b2(dkon brtsegs): gsal ba'i mche ba mdzes shing rdul mi mnga'//ngang pa'i khyu nas ngang pa'i rgyal po ni//rnam dag dkar zhing rab tu dkar ba bzhin//bde bar gsheg ba'i ljags kyi zhal khebs te/
8b3(dkon brtsegs): ud dpal dmar po rnam dag zangs kyi mdog//nyi ma char tsha nor bu dag mdog bzhin//srab la ring zhing skyon med shin du yang //'go'u ta ma yi snyen ni rab tu legs//
8b4(dkon brtsegs): seng ge'i 'grams pa seng ge 'jigs med bzhin//tshangs bcas 'jig rten lha dang mi la yang //bdag gi gang yang ma mthos pa mthong ngo //bde gshegs mthu yis mgur na mtho'
8b5(dkon brtsegs): ba ni//mgur gyi med pa gtsang la 'od gsal te//drang po'i dbus su 'gro ba yon po gsal//ro'i ro mchog mi dang lha'i lha//dpung mgo rab tu zlum zhing thal gong rgyas//
8b6(dkon brtsegs): bdun po mtho ba 'jig rten gsum tu grags//mgon po mi dbang snying po dpal gyis kyang //ri'i rtse la nyi ma char ba bzhin//mtho ba gnyis ni zhabs kyi 'thil nas skyes//
8b7(dkon brtsegs): phyag las gnyis skyes thal gong gnyis skyes te//mtho ba gcig ni ltag pa nyid ni gcig/dge zhing dag te 'od gsal rab tu rgyas//bcom ldan go'u ta ma'i phyag ring ba//
8b8(dkon brtsegs): ji ltar glang po'i rgyal po'i sna bzhin te//phyag gnyis po ni btud pa ma bgyid par//pus mo gnyis su phyag gis rab tu reg/sku'i ro stod seng ge bzhin du 'phags/
9a1(dkon brtsegs): sku ni sred med bu ltar rab tu brtan//nya gro bzhin du chu zhing gab pa'i sku//bzod pa'i stobs dang ldan pa stobs po che//sku'i 
9a2(dkon brtsegs): spu rnams g.yas 'khyil gyen du phyogs//re rer skyes shing shin tu 'jam pa ste//ji ltar pad ma chu dang 'dam mi gos//de bzhin rdul dang du bas mi gos so//rta mchog 
9a3(dkon brtsegs): tu chog dul ba yi ni ci lta bar//'doms kyi sba basbubs su nub ces gda'//rla ni rab zlum smin mi' snying po bzhin//khyod kyi rje ngar byin gyis rims bzhin 
9a4(dkon brtsegs): mchis//zhabs gong dung ltar mtho zhing rting pa che//khyod kyi phyag dang zhabs ni dra bas 'brel//sor mo ring zhing sor mo nyi shu mnyam//sen mo zangs 'dra sor 
9a5(dkon brtsegs): sen me tog can//khyod kyi zhabs thil 'khor lo rab kyi mtshan//rtsa bas stong ldan rab tu 'od gsal ba//long bus long bu 'thab par mi 'gyur zhing //gom pa 
9a6(dkon brtsegs): dag ni sa la yong mi 'dor//sa la kun tu sor bzhis mi reg par//'jig rten rnams kyi gtso bo rab gshegs te//khyod ni gang du gom pa bor bar yang //rin chen 
9a7(dkon brtsegs): pad ma skye ba ngo mtshar to//rgod par ma gyur la zhing rten par gshegs//stag dang lha dbang 'gros kyis gzhan gnon cing //bag tsha med par stabs ni bgyings te gshegs//
9a8(dkon brtsegs): dpa' bo khyod kyi gros 'dra su la 'ang med//mi ma lags ba rnams kyi bar snang las//lha'i me tog 'char ni rab 'bebs shing //klu dbyangs sgras ni khyod la mchod par bgyid//
9b1(dkon brtsegs): cho 'phrul 'di 'dra 'di dag rmad du byung //khyod kyi gzugs ni rnam thos bu las lhag//gzi rjid ni nyi ma brgya bas 'phags//tshangs bcas lha mir bcas pa'i 'jig//
9b2(dkon brtsegs): rten na//khyod 'dra 'ang med na lhag pa ga la mchis//bdag gis khyod kyi cho 'phrul mchog mthong nas//yon tan lhag pa gang dag gzigs gyur na//de bzhid pas na mi 
9b3(dkon brtsegs): dbang rab byung snyam//bdag ni de la the tshoms 'tshal bar 'gyur//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis khyim bdag tshangs dpen bzang skyong la 'di skad ces bka' stsal to//
9b4(dkon brtsegs): kye ma 'o khyim bdag 'jig rten gnas pa 'di ni brtse ba bcus brtsa se mthong ste/bcu gang zhe na 'di lta ste/skye bas gtse ba dang /rga bas gtse ba dang /'chi bas 
9b5(dkon brtsegs): gtse ba dang /mya ngan gyis gtse ba dang /smre sngags 'don pas gtse ba dang /sdug bsngal gyis gtse ba dang /yid mi bde bas gtse ba dang /'khrug pas gtse ba dang /
9b6(dkon brtsegs): 'khor bar gtse ba dang /bcu ste/khyim bdag 'jig rten gnang ba dang /gtse ba 'di bcus gtse ba mthong nas/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub 
9b7(dkon brtsegs): thob par bya'o//zhes dad pas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu byung ngo //de nas bcom ldan 'da'as kyis de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so//'khor ba'i sgrom du 
9b8(dkon brtsegs): chud pa yi//byis pa so so'i skye bo kun//skye bas nye bar gtses pa dang //na dang rga bas rab tu gzer//mya ngan byed cing smre sngags 'don//'chi ba'i dbang du song mthong nas/
10a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /srid pa'i sgrom las thar bya zhes//skyabs pa'i phyir ni mngon par byung /skye ma'o//khyim bdag 'jig rten gnas pa ni 
10a2(dkon brtsegs): zhe sdang zhing kun nas snar sems kyi dngos po bcus gcig mnar sems su byed par mthong ste bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/bdag nyid la gnod pa byas shes sems la kun nas 
10a3(dkon brtsegs): mnar sems skye ba dang /gnod par byed do zhes la kun nas mnar sems skye ba dang /gnod pa byed par 'gyur ro zhes sems la kun nas mnar semasaskye ba dang /bdag 
10a4(dkon brtsegs): gis mdza' bo la gnod par byas so zhes sems skye ba dang /bdag gis mdza' bo la (ga)nod pa byed do zhes sems la kun nas ma nar sems skye ba dang /bdag gis mdza' 
10a5(dkon brtsegs): bo la gnod pa byed par 'gyur rozhes sems la kun nas mnanar sems skye ba dang /bdagadang mi 'dza' ba'i don byas so zhes sems la kun nas (  )mnar sems skye ba 
10a6(dkon brtsegs): dang /bdag dang mi 'dza' ba'i don byed do zhes sems la kunanas mnar sems skye ba dang/bdag dang mi 'dza' ba'o don byas so zhes sems (  )la kun 
10a7(dkon brtsegs): nas mnar Ssems bskye ba dang /bdag dang mi 'dza ba'i don byed par 'gyur ro zhes sems (la)kun nas mnar sems skye ( )ba dang /gnod pa'i rab tu sdang 
10a8(dkon brtsegs): ba nyid dad bcu 'o//khyim bdag kun nas mnar sems kyi dngos po bcu po ni de dag yin te/kun nas mnar sems thams cad sel ba/bla na med pa yang dag pa(ra)
10b1(dkon brtsegs): rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob par bya'o//zhes dad pas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu byung ngo //de nas bcom ldan 'das kyi don nyid rab tu bstan pa'i phyir de'i tshigs 
10b2(dkon brtsegs): su bcad pa 'di dag gsungaso//kye ma 'o//sems can zhes sdang ste//gcig la gcig ni phan tshun du//kun nas mnar sems dngos po bcus//kun la kun 
10b3(dkon brtsegs): nas mnar sems gyur//bdag dang bdag gi nye du la//gnod pa byas dang byed pa dang //gnod pa bye par 'gyur ro zhe//de'i kun nas mnar sems skye//gang rnams bdag 
10b4(dkon brtsegs): dang mi mdza ba//de dag gis ni don byas dang //byed do byed par 'gyur ro zhes//de sI kun nas mnar sems skye //gnod pa'i sdang dang bcu yin te//gang gis mnar sems 
10b5(dkon brtsegs): 'phel gyur pa//de mthong kun nas mnar sems rnams//mi 'dod pas ni rab tu byung /kye ma 'e 'jig rten gnas pa 'di ni lta bar byas pa//lta ba ngan pa thibs po bcus lta 
10b6(dkon brtsegs): bar bya ba sna tshogs dang /lta ba ngan pa thibs po dag gis non par mthong ste/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/bdag tu lta bar gyur pa lta ba ngan pa thibs po dang //sems can du lta bar 
10b7(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur pa lta ba ngan pa thibs//srog tu lta bar gyur pa lta ngan pa thibs po dang /gang zag tu lta bar gyur pa lta gang na thibs po dang /chad par lta bar gyur pa lta ba nga na pa thibs 
10b8(dkon brtsegs): po dang /rtag par lta ba ngan pa thibs po da nga /bya ba med par lta bar gyur pa ltar ba ngan pa thibs po dang/rgyu med par lta bar gyur ba lta ngan pa thibs po da nga/mi mthun pa'i rgyur pha 
11a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /lta bar gyur ba lta ba ngan pa thibs po dang /log par lta bar gyur pa lta ba ngan pa thibs po dag bcu 'o//khyim bdag lta bar gyur lta ba ngan 
11a2(dkon brtsegs): pa thibs po de dag yin te/lta ba thams cad bzhig pa'i phyir/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob par bya'o zhes dad pas khyim nas khyims (pa'i)med par rab tu byung ngo //
11a3(dkon brtsegs): de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don 'di nyid rgya cher bstan pa'i phyir/de'i tshe tshigs su bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so//byis pa so so'i kye bo kun//bdag tu lta dang sems can lta/
11a4(dkon brtsegs): srog tu blta ba rnams kyi yang //lta ba'i thib po rab tu g.yogs//rtag dang chad par lta ba dang //bya med lta la gnas pa dang //yang dag lta bar dgod do zhes//de phyir nga 
11a5(dkon brtsegs): yin rab tu byung //kye ma'o khyim bdag 'jig rten gnas pa 'di ni zug rngu dang //'dod chags kyi zug du chen po bcus//bskal pa grangs med pa bye ba khrag khrig brgya stong du zug rngu 
11a6(dkon brtsegs): zug par mthong ste/bcu gang zhen//'di lta ste/srid pa'i zug rngu dang /ma rig pa'i zug rngu dang /'dod pa'i zug rngu dang /'don chags kyi zug rngu dang /zhe sdang gi zug rngu dang /gti mugi zug rngu dang /
11a7(dkon brtsegs): nga rgyal gyi zug rngu dang /lta ba'i zug rngu dang /'byung ba'i zug rngu dang /'jig pa'i zug rngu dang bcu'o//khyim bdag zug rngu chen po bcu po ni de dag yin te/zug rngu thams cad 
11a8(dkon brtsegs): gsal (ba'i)phyir/bla na med pa yang dag par rdzogs pa'i byang chub thob par bya'o//zhes dad pas khyim nas khyim med par rab tu byung ngo//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis de'i tshe tshigs

25a2(dkon brtsegs): pa'i chos de rtogs par gyis shig/de ci'i phyir zhe na/khyim bdag rnams rnam pa thams cad du mi dmigs su med pa'o//khyim bdag rnams rdul dang bral ba'i phyir chos 
25a3(dkon brtsegs): thams cad bdag med pa'o//khyim bdag dang bral ba'i phyir chos thams cad ni sems can med pa'o//khyim bdag rams skye ba dang /rga ba dang /na ba dang /
25a4(dkon brtsegs): 'chi ba dang /mya ngan dang /smre sngags 'don pa dang /sdug bsngal ba dang /yid mi bde ba dang /'khrug pa las shin du 'das pa'i phyir /chos thams cad ni srog med pa'o//
25a5(dkon brtsegs): khyim bdag rnams dus gsum yang dag par 'tshad pa'i phyir /chos thams cad ni zag pa med pa'o//khyim bdag rnams sgra dang skad dang /dbyangs dang tshigi lam nas brjod 
25a6(dkon brtsegs): du med pa'i phyir chos thams cad ni yi ge med pa'o//khyim bdag snams dmigs pa med pa'i phyir chos thams cad ni chags pa med pa'o//khyim bad rnams nye bar zhi ba'i 
25a7(dkon brtsegs): mtshan nyid phyir chos thams cad ni zhi ba 'o//khyim bdag rnams namakha'i rang bzhin gyi phyir chos thams cad nikun du song ba'o//khyim bdag rnams nges par 'byung ba ma 
25a8(dkon brtsegs): yin pa'i phyir chos thams cad stong pa nyid la yang dag par gnas pa'o//khyim bdag rnams gnas pa'i phyir chos thams cadani ma g.yos pa'o//khyim bdag rnoms ni gnas shing|
25b1(dkon brtsegs): g.yo ba med pa dang ldan pa'i phyir/chos thams cad ni yang dag pa'i mtha' la gnas pa'o//khyim bdag rnams dpa' klong dang bral ba'i phyir/chos thams cad nibarjod 
25b2(dkon brtsegs): du med pa'o//khyim bdag rnam mtshan ma med/dbyibs med pa sna rgyu ba dang bral ba'i phyir//chos thams cad ni kha dog med pa'o//khyim bdag dang rnams nga yir 'dzin pa 
25b3(dkon brtsegs): pa dang bral ba'i phyir chos thams cad ni nga'i ba med pa'o//khyim bdag rams sems dang yid dang rnam par shes pa dang ma'i phyir/chos thams cad shes par bya ba ma yin pa'o//
25b4(dkon brtsegs): khyim bdag rnams mig gi lam las shin du 'das pa dang /rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid las shin du 'das pa'i phyir//chos thams 
25b5(dkon brtsegs): cad ni kun bzhi med pa'o//khyim bdag gnas skye ba dang /'jig pa dang /gnas pa dang bral ba'i phyir chos pa thams cad ni dor ba med pa snan pa med pa'o//khyim 
25b6(dkon brtsegs): bdag rnams sems dang yid dang rams par shes pa dang bral ba'i phyir/chos thams cad ni byed pa g.yo ba med pa'o//khyim ba dag rnams rang bzhin gyis nyams 
25b7(dkon brtsegs): chung ba'i phyir chos thams cadani rkyen las rag las pa'o//khyim bdag rnams mig ni 'byung ba chen po bzhi las gyur pa mi rtag pa/mi rtan pa dang /ther zug ma 
25b8(dkon brtsegs): yin pa/snying po med pa/nyams chung zhigs pa//yun thung ba/yid rten du mi rung ba/sdug bsngal ba nad mang ba/brtse ba mang ba yin te/
26a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /khyim bdag rnams kyis mig de la gnas par mi bya'o//khyim bdag rnams kyi mig de la gnas par mi
26a2(dkon brtsegs): bya'o//khyim bdag rnams rnam ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yang ni 'byung ba chen po bzhi las gyur pa/mi rtag pa ther zug ma yin pa/
26a3(dkon brtsegs): yid brtan du mi rung ba/snying po med pa/zhigs pa/nad can/sdug bsngal ba/nad mang ba/rtsa ba mang ba yin te/khyim ba dag khyed rnams kyis 
26a4(dkon brtsegs): yid de la gnas par mi bya'o/ /yang khyim bdag rnams 'di ltar brlab bar bya ste/mig 'di ni gnyer mi bzod pa dbu ba lta bu'o//mig 'di ni ring du mi gnas te/
26a5(dkon brtsegs): chu'i chu bur lta bu'o//mig 'di ni las dang nyon mongs pa dang /srid pa las byung ba ste/mig sgyu lta bu'o//mig 'di ni rang bzhin kyi snying po med de 
26a6(dkon brtsegs): chu shing lta bu'o//mig 'di ni phyin ci log las byung te/sgyu ma lta bu 'o//mig 'di ni leg pa dang yang dag pa ma yin pa/mthong ba ste smi lam lta 
26a7(dkon brtsegs): bu'o//mig 'di ni rkyen la rag las pa ste/brag chaI lta bu'o//mig 'di ni lam kyi mig yor gyi kun tu snang ba ste mig g.yor lta bu'o//mig 'dini 
26a8(dkon brtsegs): sems 'khrug pa med par 'gyur ba'i mtshan nyid de sbrin lta bu'o//mig 'di ni skad cig tu 'jig pa dang ldan te glog lta bu'o//mig 'di ni bdag po med de 
26b1(dkon brtsegs): sa dang mtshungs so//mig 'di ni bdag med de chu dang mtshungs so//mig 'ad ni sems can med de me dang mtshungs so//mig 'a di ni srog me de rlung dang mtshungs 
26b2(dkon brtsegs): so//mig 'di ni gang zag med de nam mkha' dang mtshungs so//mig  'di ni 'byung ba chen po'i kun bzhi ste yang dag pa ma yin pa'o//mig 'di ni 
26b3(dkon brtsegs): bdag dang bdag gi dang bral ba ste stong pa'o//mig 'di ni rtsa dang rtsig pa dang /shing dang rdo dang /mig yor lta bu ste/bems po lta bu'o//
26b4(dkon brtsegs): mig 'di ni rlung 'khrul 'khor dang ldan par 'byung ste byed pa med pa'o//mig 'di ni bsod ste/mi gtsang ba dang rna ba bsags pa'o//mig 'di ni 
26b5(dkon brtsegs): gsob ste g.yog pa kun tu mnye ba/gzhig pa rnam par thor ba 'jig pa'i chos can no//mig 'di ni rtag tu dga' ba'i zil gyis gnon pa ste/khron 
26b6(dkon brtsegs): pa snyings pa dang mtshungs so//mig 'i ni mthar 'chi ba ste mthar mi gnas pa'o//khyim bdag rnams mig ci lta ba de bzhin du/rna ba dang /sna dang /lce 
26b7(dkon brtsegs): dang /lus dang /yid kyang rig par bya'o//gong ma bzhin du sbyar te/chos thams cad kyi bar bya'o//khyim bdag rnams gzhan yang 'di 
26b8(dkon brtsegs): lta ste/mig dang /rna ba dang /sna dang lce dang /lus 'di ni byis pa so so'i skye bo thams cad kyi 'dod pa tsam mo//'di lta ste yid 'di ni byis 
27a1(dkon brtsegs): pa so so'i skye bo thams cad kyi 'dod pa nam mo//'di lta ste/gzugs 'di dang /'di lta ste/
27a2(dkon brtsegs): sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg bya dang /chos 'di ni byis pa so so'i skye bo thams can kyi 'dod pa tsam mo//khyim bdag rnams gzugs kyi phung po 'di ni 
27a3(dkon brtsegs): byis pa so so'i skye bo thamasacad kyi 'dod pa tsam mo//'di lta ste khyim bdag rnams tshor ba'i phung po 'di ni byis pa so so'i skya bo thams cad kyis 'dod pa tsam mo//
27a4(dkon brtsegs): 'di lta ste khyim bdag rnam 'dus shes kyi phung po 'di ni byis so so'i skye bo thams cadakyi 'dod pa tsam mo//'di lta ste khyim bdag rnam
27a5(dkon brtsegs): ni byis pa so so'i skye bo thangs cad kyi 'dod pa tsam mo//'di lta ste khyim bdag rams rnam par shes pa'i phung po 'di ni byis ba so so'i skye bo thams cad
27a6(dkon brtsegs): pa tsam mo//'di lta ste khyem bdag rnams sa'i khams 'di ni byis pa so so skye bo thams cad kyi 'dod pa tsam po//'di lta ste khyim bag rnams chu'i khams 
27a7(dkon brtsegs): dang /ma'i khams dang /rlung gi khams 'di ni byis pa sos skye ba thams cad kyi 'dod pa can mo//'di lta ste khyims bdag rnams 'dus byas dang /'dus ma 
27a8(dkon brtsegs): byas shes byas ba'i chos 'di ni byis pa so so'i skye bo thams cad kyi 'do pa tsam mo//de bas na khyims bdag khyed rnams kyi mi 'dod par gyis shig/mi 'dod 
27b1(dkon brtsegs): par gyur nas bu chung ma rnams nye bar ma len cig /nor rnams ma len cig /de dag mi len cig /de dag mi lan par gyur nas dad pas khyim nas khyim med par 
27b2(dkon brtsegs): rab tu byung bar gyis shig /dad pas khyims nas khyim med par rab tu byung nas mi 'dod par gyis shig/khyim bdag rnams rab tu byung ba'i mi 'dod pa gang 
27b3(dkon brtsegs): zhe na/gang so so thar ba'i sdom pas bsdams shing /cho ga dad spyod yul phun sum tshogs pa kha na ma tho ba rdul tsam la yang 'jig par lta zhing /bslab 
27b4(dkon brtsegs): pa'i bzhi yang dag par bzung nas slob te/tshul khrims dang ldan par gnas pa'i tshul khrims kyi phung bo dang ldan pa ni/mig mi len/na ba mi len 
27b5(dkon brtsegs): sna mi len/lce dang /lus dang /yid mi len/gzugs mi len/sgra dang /dri dang /rol dang /reg bya dang /chos mi len/gzugs dang /tshor ba 
27b6(dkon brtsegs): dang /'du shes dang /'du byed dang /rnam par shes pa'i phung po mi len/sa'i khams mi len/chu'i khams mi len/me'i khams dang /rlung gi khams dang /
27b7(dkon brtsegs): nam mkha'i khams dang /rnam par shes pa'i+i khams mi len te/khyim bdag rnams 'di ni rab tu byung ba mi 'dod pa'o//mig gi khams mi len to//gong 
27b8(dkon brtsegs): ma bzhin du sbyar te/yid kyi khams kyi bar du mi len to//gong ma bzhin du sbyar te/gang mi len pa de yongs su mi spong ngo //ci yongasu mi spong zhe na/mig yongasu 
28a1(dkon brtsegs): mi spong /rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid yongs su mi spongs /gzugs yongs su 
28a2(dkon brtsegs): mi spong /sgra dang /dri dang /ro dang /reg bya dang /chos yongs su mi spong /gzugs kyi phung po yongs su mi spong /tshor ba'i phung po yongs su mi spong/'dus shes 
28a3(dkon brtsegs): kyi phung po yongs su mi spog /'du byed kyi phung po yongs su mi sbong /rnam par shes pa'i phung po yongs su mi spyo/sa'i khams yongs su mi spong /chu'i khams dang /me'i 
28a4(dkon brtsegs): khams dang /rlung gi khams dang /nam mkha'i khams dang /rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi bar du yongs su mi spong /de nas mi spong pa de kun nas nyon mongs par mi 
28a5(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur ro//kun nas nyon mongs pa med na yang bar 'gyur ro//de rnams yang bar gyu pa de ni /de mi snang bar 'gyur ro//de nam mi snang de ni gang la yang khong 
28a6(dkon brtsegs): khro bar mi bya'o//de nam khro bar mi byed pa de ni bdag la gnod par mi sems/gzhan la gnod par mi sems/gnyis ga la gnod par mi sems te/de gnod 
28a7(dkon brtsegs): par mi sems pas/phung po lhag me med pa'i mya ngan las 'das pa'i dbyings su yongs su mya ngan las 'da' 'o//khyim bdag rnams de la gang yongs su mya 
28a8(dkon brtsegs): ngan las 'das zhe na/de la mig yongs su mya ngan las mi 'da'/rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid yongs su mya ngan las 
28b1(dkon brtsegs): mi 'da' mod kyi/khyim bdag rnams gang mig la rten nas/nga zhe 'am/nga'i zhes yang dag par ma yin pa 'dzin pa de/dang /bral ba ni yongs su mya ngan 
28b2(dkon brtsegs): las mi 'da' ba'o//gang dang bral ba yongs su/mya ngan las 'das pa yin zhe na/'dod chags dang bral ba ni yongs su mya nganalas 'das pa'o//zhe sdang 
28b3(dkon brtsegs): dang bral ba ni yongs su myi ngan las 'das pa'o//gti mug dang bral ba ni yongs su ma ngan las 'das pa'o//mi shes pa dang bral ba ni yongs su mi ngan las 'das pa ste/
28b4(dkon brtsegs): yang khyim bdag rnams mi shes pa ni/'das pa la med par ma gyur/da ltar dang ma 'ongs pa la/med par ma gyur mod gyi/mi shes pa dang bral nas shes pa skyes 
28b5(dkon brtsegs): so//khyim bdag rnams de la shes pa gang zhe na/'di lta ste zad pa shes pa'o//de la zad pa shes pa gang zhe na/zad pa shes pa ni 'das pa ma yin/zad 
28b6(dkon brtsegs): pa shes pa ni ma 'ong pa ma yin/zad pa shes pa ni da ltar byung ba ma yin mod kyis/khyim bdag rnams gang la brten te mi shes pa dang bral nas/shes pa skye bar 
28b7(dkon brtsegs): 'gyur ba de ni mi shes pa dang bral ba'i shes pa'o//mig la rten nas mi shes pa dang bral zhing /shes pa skyes kyang /mig ni nga'i ma yin pa de ni yongs su 
28b8(dkon brtsegs): bzung ba'o//mig yongs su bzung ba de spangs pa'o//gang spangs pa de grol ba'o//gang la grol zhe na bdag du 'dzin pa las grol/sems can 
29a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /du 'dzin pa las grol/srog tu 'dzin pa dang /gang zag tu 'dzin pa dang /chad par

29a3(dkon brtsegs): par mi rtog go/ci la mi rtog ce na/nga dang nga'i zhes mi rtog te/de rtsegs shing rtsogs par mi byed la spong zhing mi len to/spangs pas yongs su mya ngan las 'das 
29a4(dkon brtsegs): so//grol lo byung ngo rab tu rnam par grol lo//bral lo gang las byung zhes na/sdug bsngal thams cad las byung ngo //khyim bdag khyed rnams 'byung bar 'dod 
29a5(dkon brtsegs): na/chos gang yang blang bar mi bya'o//de ci'i phyir zhe na/khyim bdag rnams len pas na srid par 'gyur ro//mi len pa srid pa med do//de la khyim bdag 
29a6(dkon brtsegs): rnams mig yongs su mya ngan las 'da' pa ma yin no//gong ma bzhin du sbyar te/rna ba dang /sna dang /lce dang /lus dang /yid kyi bar du yongs 
29a7(dkon brtsegs): su mya ngan las 'das pa ma yin pas na/rnam par shes pa'i khams kyi bar du yongs su mya ngan las 'ad pa sam yin mod kyi/khyim bdag rnams gang rnam par shes 
29a8(dkon brtsegs): pa brten te/da dang da'i zhes yang dag par ma yin par 'dzin pa de dang bral ba de ni yongasu mya ngan las 'das pa'o//gang dang bral na yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa
29b1(dkon brtsegs): yin zhe na/'dod chags dang bral ba ni yongs su mya ngan las 'das pa'o//zhe sdang dang bral ba ni yongs su myi ngan las 'das pa'o/gti mug dang bral ba ni

29b4(dkon brtsegs): pa shes pa gang zhe na/zad pa shes pa ni 'das pa ma yin/zad pa shes pa ma 'ongs pa ma yin/zad pa shesapa ni da ltar byung ba ma yin mod kyis/khyim bdag rnams mig 
29b5(dkon brtsegs): la rten te/mi shes pa dang bral na shes pa skyes par gyur pa de ni mi shes pa dang bral ba'i shes pa'o//mig la brten te mi shes pa dang bral nas/shes pa skyes kyang /shes 
29b6(dkon brtsegs): pa'i dbyings ni da'i ba ma yin no//gang da'i ba ma yin pa de yongs su ma bzung 'o//gang yongs su bzung ba de spangs pa'o//gang spangs pa de grol ba'o//gang las 
29b7(dkon brtsegs): grol zhe na/bdag tu 'dzin pa las grol/sems can du 'dzin pa dang /srog tu 'dzin dang /gang zag tu 'dzin pa dang /chad par 'dzin pa dang /rtag 
29b8(dkon brtsegs): par 'dzin pa las grol/yongs su rtog pa las grol/de yongs su mi rtog go/yongs su mi rtog go/yongasasu mi rtog pas mi rtog rnam par mi rtog go/ci

30a6(dkon brtsegs): pas de ni mi len to//lam gang nyid la bsgom bya ba//shes rab kyi ni de lta ste//shes rab kyi ni mthong 'gyur bas//rnam pa gzhan du mi 'gyur ro//
30a7(dkon brtsegs): skye mched thams cad stong pa ste//snying po med pa g.yo zhing g.yog/skye dgu rnams ni sred pa bslus//de la chags par mi bya'o//chos rnams

30b1(dkon brtsegs): chos rnams kun//stong pa nyid du shes pa ste//sdug bsngal dag ni kun las mthar//de las brtsod pa med pa yin//'dod pa 'di ni yongs su

30b3(dkon brtsegs): las ni rdzogs skye skye ba ste//de ni 'jug pa dag dang 'grub par 'gyur//skyes gnas nad dang rga dang 'chi ba dang /mi 'dod sdug bsngal chen po gzhan dag gnon//

30b5(dkon brtsegs): kun gcig du 'dus 'gyur te//yongs su 'dzin pa rnam par spang byos la//bu dang chung ma dag la dga' spongs ste//dge slong tshul 'di myur du blang bar byos//shes 
30b6(dkon brtsegs): kyi khyed 'dod rnyed pa 'dod ma byed//ngan ngon tsam dag gi ni chos byos la//gdol pa dag dang 'dra ba'i dma' sems kyis//gzhan gyi grong du nye bar bya//'gro ba'i 'gro bar bya//da ni tshul 
30b7(dkon brtsegs): khrims ldan zhes bdag ma snyems//'di ni tshul khrims ngan ces gzhan ma brnyas//tshul khrims tsam gyis gzhan la gang brnyas pa//snyas phyir de ni tshul khrims 'chal

31a8(dkon brtsegs): sgyu ma lta bur snang //ngas ni nang dang phyi rol chos rnams kun//stong pa nyid de sgyu ma 'dra bar mkhyer//byis pa mi shes log par 'dzin pa dang //nga dang 
31b1(dkon brtsegs): nga'i zhes kyang rnam par rtog//mig ni nang na cung zad yod ma yin//phyi rol du'ang sems can mi dmigs te//mig ni bdag med byed srog med pa de//chos 
31b2(dkon brtsegs): rnams kun kyang de ltar shes par byos//mig ni sems shing grol bar 'dod ma yin//de bzhin rna ba dang ni sna dang lce//lus dang yid dang gzugs dang byed med de//

53b2(dkon brtsegs): ngo mtshar dang smad du shes bskyed de/bcu gang zhe na/'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bsam gyi mi khyab pa la mngon par dad/

53b4(dkon brtsegs): gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhIn gshegs pa'i dbyangs bsam gyis mi khyab pa mngon par dad pa nas ngo mtshar smad du 'du shesabaskyed pa'i bar du 'o//'di lta ste/
53b5(dkon brtsegs): de bzhin gshegs pa'i ye shes bsam gyis mi khyab pa dang /'di lta ste/de bzhin pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshes pa'i 'od bsam gyis mi khyab

53b8(dkon brtsegs): bzhin gshegs pa'i mi 'jigs pa rnams bsam gyis mi khyab pa dag/'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshags pa'i snying rje chen po bsam 
55a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /gyis mi khyab pa dang /'di lta ste de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i sangs

55a3(dkon brtsegs): 'du shes bskyed ba'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i ngo mtshar dang rmad kyi chos bcu po 'di rnams kyi phyir/brtson 'grus tsher rtsom zhing mi zhum mi 'gong mi skyo te|
55a4(dkon brtsegs): de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsam gyis mi khyab pa ngo mtshar dang smad kyi chos bcu po ma thob gyi bar du|lus kyi lpags pa dang /rgyus pa dang /phyi dbugs dang /
55a5(dkon brtsegs): rus pa dag zhig la/lus kyi sha dang khrag skams kyang bla'i brtson 'grus nyams par mi bya'o//snyam du sems bskyed do//sha ri'i bu de ltar dad pas mos pI 
55a6(dkon brtsegs): byang chub sems dpa' ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i bsamagyi mi khyab ba'i ngo mtshar dang rmad kyi tshes bcu po 'di rnams la mngon par dad /yid ches rab tu rtog/
55a7(dkon brtsegs): the tshom mi za rgya cher dga' mguste rangs par rnyed do//de nas bcom ldan 'das kyis don 'di nyid rgya cher rab tu bstan pa'i phyir de'i tshe tshigasu bcad pa 'di dag gsungs so//
55a8(dkon brtsegs): sangs rgyas sku ni bsam gyi mi khyab//chos kyi sku yi rab tu phyed //mtshan ma med cing snang med la//rgyal ba'i thugs sras dad par byed//'gro ba tha dad ci snyed kyi//sgra skad 
55b1(dkon brtsegs): bsams kyi mi khyab pa//thams cad du ni chos sgrog ste//sangs rgyas kyi ni spyod yul dang //ci tsam sems can thams cad kyi//dus gsum dag gi dbang po 
55b2(dkon brtsegs): kun//sangs rgyas kyis ni rab mkhyen te//bsam mi khyab la bar du dang //sangs rgyas rnams kyi 'od mtha' yas//'od zer dra ba bsam mi khyab//zhing 
55b3(dkon brtsegs): rnams rgya tsho mtha' yas pa//thams cad kyi ni phyogs su khyab//thub pa'i tshul khrims 'jig rten 'das//'jig rten chos la mi gnas te//rdzu 'phrul rkang pa

55b6(dkon brtsegs): bzhin du mtha' yas pa//sems can gcig gi don phyir yang //bskal pa rgya mtsho mtha' yas par//'dul ba'i don phyir bzhugs pas na//de dag snying rje de 'dra'o//

55b8(dkon brtsegs): cad mchog ldan pa//chos rnams thams cad thugs su chung //sangs rgyas chos rnams ma 'dris pa//thams cad du ni ye shes gzigs//dad pa la ni rab gnas pa//
56a1(dkon brtsegs): sangs rgyas kun chos chos nyid rnams//bsam gyi mi khyab 'di dag la//de dag thams cad dad par byed//
56a2(dkon brtsegs): sha ri'i bu ji ltar byang chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bsam gyi mi khyab pa la mngon par dad/yid ches rab tu rtog/the tsom mi za/

56b2(dkon brtsegs): pas/yon tan thams cad rab tu rgyas pa'o//'di lta ste de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ni yongs su dag pa'i me long gi dkyil 'khor gyi gzugs snyan lta lta bu dang /'di

56b4(dkon brtsegs): pa'i sku ni zag pa thams cad las log pas zag pa med pa'o//'di lta ste/de bzhin gshegs pa sku ni 'dus byas thams cad dang bral bas 'dus ma byas pa'o//
56b5(dkon brtsegs): nam mkha' lta bu'i sku'o//mnyam pa med pa'i sku'o//'jig rten gsum po thams cad las kyang rab tu 'phags pa'i sku'o//de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku dpe med 
56b6(dkon brtsegs): pa zla med pa/mtshungs pa med pa'i sku ste/dag pa dri ma med pa/kun nas nyon mongs pa med pa rang bzhin gyi 'od gsal ba'o//des ni sngon gyis mtha'

57b3(dkon brtsegs): byung ba'i sku'o//mi 'gyur ba'i sku'o//mi 'khrugs pa'i sku'o//rnam par mi rtogs pa'i sku'o//mi gnas pa'i sku'o//mi snyems pa'i sku'o//gnas

58a7(dkon brtsegs): pa dang /snying rje brgya chen pa bskyed nas sbyin pa ma byin/'dod la log par g.yem pa dag spong shignam mkha' lta bu mchog gi sku tshol lo//gser 
58a8(dkon brtsegs): ltar dag cing mdzes pa'i gos rnams ni/dpag tu med pa gang gi 'bye ma snyed/sbyin pa'i pha rol tu phyin pa dag spyod cing /rgyal ba'i sras kyis 'jig

58b5(dkon brtsegs): bdag dang sems can bral mtshan ma med cing sgra yang bral ba thob/mig gi yul kun las ni rab 'das pa/de 'dri'i sku ni thub pas yang dag thob/bzod 
58b6(dkon brtsegs): ma nas stong ma byas ma byung ba/gzugs dang sgra bral yid kyang rnam dag pa/de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku ma g.yo gang thong ba/de ni phyogs bcu'i bde bar gshegs 
58b7(dkon brtsegs): pa mthong /ji ltar sgyu ma sprul pa sna tshogs lus/rta dang glang po bud med skyes bu'i gzugs/pus pa slu ba brdzun zhing phyin ci log/de bzhin 
58b8(dkon brtsegs): stobs bcu rnams la lta bar byos/gang dag 'das pa'i bde bar gshegs dpag med dang /ma byon rgyal ba de bzhin gang snang dag/chos nyid gcig gyur mnyam pa 
59a1(dkon brtsegs): med pa'i sku/chos kyi dbyings kyi klas shing nam mkha' 'dra/de ltar byang chub sems dpa' 
59a2(dkon brtsegs): de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i sku bsam gyi mi khyab la la mngon par dad/yid ches rab tu rtogs/the tsom mi za/rgya cher

59a7(dkon brtsegs): 'di/dge bsnyen gyi 'khor la ni 'di/bram ze'i 'khor la ni 'di/rgyal rigs kyi 'khor la ni 'di/khyim bdag gi 'khor la ni 'di/lha'i 'khor la ni 
59a8(dkon brtsegs): 'di/tshangs pa'i 'khor la ni 'di bshad ste/'di lta ste/mdo'i sde dang /dbyangs kyi snyed pa'i sngo dang /lung bstan pa'i sde dang /tshigs su bcad pa'i 
59b1(dkon brtsegs): sde dang /mched du brjod pa'i sde dang /gleng gzhi' sde dang /rtogs pa brjod pa'i sde dang /de lta bu byung ba'i sde dang /skyes pa rabs kyi sde dang /shin du 
59b2(dkon brtsegs): rgyas pa'i sde dang /rmad du byung pa'i chos kyi sde dang /rten la dbab pa gtan pa'i sde bshad par bya'o//snyam du mi dgongs mod kyi/'on kyang shi ri'i bu

59b6(dkon brtsegs): dbyangs ni de bzhin gshegs pa'i sngon gyis bsod nams kyi 'bras bu rnam par smin pa las mngon par grub pa gden pa'i rjes su 'brangs bas 'jug go/yang sha 
59b7(dkon brtsegs): ri'i bu de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs de ni 'jam par 'byung ba dang /yid du 'ong ba dang /yid dga' ba dang /dag pa dag dri ma med pa dang /snyan pa dang /
59b8(dkon brtsegs): mnyan par 'os pa dang /shes par bya bar 'os pa dang /mi rtsub pa dang /mi brlab pa dang /lus sim par byed pa dang /sems tshim par byed pa dang /
60a1(dkon brtsegs): @#/ O /snying dga' bar byed pa dang /dga' phye dang/bde ba bskyed pa dang /kun shes par byed pa dang /gsal

60a4(dkon brtsegs): gnyan pa dang /zur phyin pa dang /bsgrags pa dang /sems can thams cad kyi dbang po mgu bar byed pa dang /'khor thams cad du grags pa dang /
60a5(dkon brtsegs): rnam pa thams cad du mchog dang ldan pa dang /de dag dang gzhan yon tan dpag tu med pa dang ldan pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa'i gsungs dbyang ste/sha ri'i 
60a6(dkon brtsegs): bu 'di ni de bzhin gshegs pa rnams kyi de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs bsam gyis mi khyab pa ste/de la byang chub sems dpa' mngon par dad/yid ches    
60a7(dkon brtsegs): rab tu('a)rtogs/the tsom mi za/rgya chen dga' mgu ste/rangs pa rnyed nas ngo mtshar dang rmad (du)'du shes bskyed do//'di la chos nyid ni 'di yin ste//
60a8(dkon brtsegs): de la 'di ('a)skad ces bya'o//tshangs pa yi ni sgra dbyangs su/'dren pa gsung sgra rab 'byin te/tshangs pa rnams ni tshim par byed/'di 'dra'i chos ni phun 
60b1(dkon brtsegs): sum tshogs/de'i dbyangs ni byams dang ldan/snying rje las ni yang dag 'byung /gtang snyoms dang ni dga' ldan pa/thus pa'i dbyangs 
60b2(dkon brtsegs): ni rab tu 'byung /srog chags kun gyi 'dod chags med/zhe sdang du ni zhi byed cing /gti mug pa de sel ba ste/phun sum tshogs dbyangs 'di 'dra'o//
60b3(dkon brtsegs): 'dzam bu gling gi sna tshogs phyogs rnams na/mir 'gyur pa'i skad ni mtha' yas pa/kun de'i sgra rnams sna tshogs dang /dbyangs 'di thos nas rjes su 
60b4(dkon brtsegs): rtogs par 'gyur/sa bla dang ni de bzhin par sna lha/de dag ci 'dra skad la 'jug pa star/de phyir thub dbang dbyangs ni rab tu zhugs te/rnam par 
60b5(dkon brtsegs): nges thos myang 'das reg 'gyur/rkang gnyis dag dang rkang bzhi rnams dang ni/rkang mangs dag dang rkang pa med pa rnams/thams cad la ni de'i sgra dbyangs 
60b6(dkon brtsegs): 'jug/bskul nas rab tu 'chos shing tshang gcod byed/sto gsum 'di yis sgra rnams ci snyed pa/tha ma dang ni 'bring dang de bzhin rab/de dag rjes su 
60b7(dkon brtsegs): 'jug dbyangs de sgrog ste/'dul ba'i sems can thar pa reg par byed/rtogs pa med cing rnam par mi rtog ste/gzung ba dang ni bcings las byung ba yin/
60b8(dkon brtsegs): mnyam par gzhag pas 'phags pa'i bden pa 'chad/sems can thos nas nyon mongs zhi bar byed/sangs rgyas chos dang de bzhin dge 'dun 
61a1(dkon brtsegs): sgra/sbyin tho tshul khrims de bzhin bzod pa dag/mtha' yas sgra ni sems can thos byed de/de bzhin gshegs 
61a2(dkon brtsegs): pa'i dbyangs ni 'di 'dra'o//dbyangs kyang mtha' yas ye shes mtha' yas pa/de'i dbyangs kyi tshad ni yod med do//de la byang chub sems 
61a3(dkon brtsegs): dpa' mkhas dad cing //de bzhin gshegs pa'i dbyangs la the tshom med/sha ri'i bu ji lta na bya chub sems dpa' de bzhin gshegs pa rnams
